# Agents · P · part 36

938 entries, project-searcher to prompt-engineer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **project-searcher**: 项目搜索专用代理（强制）。主程序需了解项目结构、搜索代码、查找文件时必须且只能调用此代理完成。严禁主程序直接使用 Grep、Glob、Read 或通过 Bash（find、grep、rg、cat、head、tail 等命令）搜索项目内容来绕过此代理。违反此规则将导致搜索结果不可复现。 ([lingxiaobc/claude_code_template](https://github.com/lingxiaobc/claude_code_template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-searcher.md))

- **project-security-reviewer**: Planning-stage security reviewer. Ingests a project design document, analyzes it against the MITRE ATT&CK framework, and returns potential vulnerabilities with suggested mitigations — grounded in organization-wide policies, past decisions, and related systems rather than the document alone. Use before a project starts building, and to decide whether a launch is low-risk enough for the owning team to self-approve. ([uygnoey/skills-from-claude-blog](https://github.com/uygnoey/skills-from-claude-blog/blob/HEAD/2026.07.21_how-anthropic-secures-its-ai-native-software-development-lifecycle/agents/project-security-reviewer.md) · ★4)
